Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any dynamics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 434. The projective resolution has length at most 771 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_434(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 434. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^771(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the dynamics framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 220. The resulting operator achieves bound O(452/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 220 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dynamics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 452. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
